#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
#include <immintrin.h>
#endif

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

namespace {

// A tile is large enough to amortize OpenMP task overhead while keeping the
// three tiles used by an update in private cache on current multicore CPUs.
constexpr size_t kBlockSize = 96;

inline double dotProduct(const double* const left, const double* const right,
                         const size_t count) {
    size_t p = 0;
    double sum = 0.0;

#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
    // Four independent accumulators hide FMA latency.  Unaligned loads are
    // intentional because tile rows need not begin on a vector boundary.
    __m256d sum0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    __m256d sum1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    __m256d sum2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    __m256d sum3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    for (; p + 16 <= count; p += 16) {
        sum0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p),
                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p), sum0);
        sum1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 4),
                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 4), sum1);
        sum2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 8),
                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 8), sum2);
        sum3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 12),
                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 12), sum3);
    }

    const __m256d vectorSum =
        _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_add_pd(sum0, sum1), _mm256_add_pd(sum2, sum3));
    const __m128d halves =
        _mm_add_pd(_mm256_castpd256_pd128(vectorSum),
                   _mm256_extractf128_pd(vectorSum, 1));
    sum = _mm_cvtsd_f64(_mm_hadd_pd(halves, halves));
#endif

    const size_t tailBegin = p;
#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
    for (size_t tail = tailBegin; tail < count; ++tail) {
        sum += left[tail] * right[tail];
    }
    return sum;
}

// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
                        const size_t end,
                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
        return;
    }

    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);

        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
            size_t noFailure = n;
            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);
            return;
        }

        rowJ[j] = std::sqrt(value);
        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];

        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
            const double dot =
                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
        }
    }
}

// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
                    const size_t rowEnd,
                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
        return;
    }

    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
                                          j - diagonalBegin);
            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
        }
    }
}

// A(rowBlock, columnBlock) -= L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) *
// L(columnBlock, diagonalBlock)^T.  Only the lower half of diagonal tiles is
// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.
void updateTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
                const size_t columnEnd,
                const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
        return;
    }

    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == columnBegin;
    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(i + 1, columnEnd)
                                               : columnEnd;
        for (size_t j = columnBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
                                          diagonalEnd - diagonalBegin);
            rowI[j] -= dot;
        }
    }
}

}  // namespace

// Blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  Explicit dependency tokens
// describe the tile dataflow to OpenMP, allowing updates from different block
// columns to execute concurrently while preserving update order for each tile.
bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    if (n == 0) {
        return true;
    }

    double* const matrix = A.data();
    const size_t blockCount = (n + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize;
    std::vector<unsigned char> tokenStorage(blockCount * blockCount);
    [[maybe_unused]] unsigned char* const dependencyTokens =
        tokenStorage.data();
    std::atomic<size_t> failureIndex(n);

#pragma omp parallel
    {
#pragma omp single
        {
            for (size_t diagonalBlock = 0; diagonalBlock < blockCount;
                 ++diagonalBlock) {
                const size_t diagonalBegin = diagonalBlock * kBlockSize;
                const size_t diagonalEnd =
                    std::min(diagonalBegin + kBlockSize, n);
                const size_t diagonalToken =
                    diagonalBlock * blockCount + diagonalBlock;

#pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd, diagonalToken)      \
    shared(matrix, dependencyTokens, failureIndex)                           \
    depend(inout : dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) priority(2)
                factorDiagonalTile(matrix, n, diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd,
                                   failureIndex);

                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
                     rowBlock < blockCount; ++rowBlock) {
                    const size_t rowBegin = rowBlock * kBlockSize;
                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBegin + kBlockSize, n);
                    const size_t panelToken =
                        rowBlock * blockCount + diagonalBlock;

#pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd, rowBegin, rowEnd,  \
                              diagonalToken, panelToken)                     \
    shared(matrix, dependencyTokens, failureIndex)                           \
    depend(in : dependencyTokens[diagonalToken])                             \
    depend(inout : dependencyTokens[panelToken]) priority(1)
                    solvePanelTile(matrix, n, diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd,
                                   rowBegin, rowEnd, failureIndex);
                }

                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
                     rowBlock < blockCount; ++rowBlock) {
                    const size_t rowBegin = rowBlock * kBlockSize;
                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBegin + kBlockSize, n);
                    const size_t rowPanelToken =
                        rowBlock * blockCount + diagonalBlock;

                    for (size_t columnBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
                         columnBlock <= rowBlock; ++columnBlock) {
                        const size_t columnBegin = columnBlock * kBlockSize;
                        const size_t columnEnd =
                            std::min(columnBegin + kBlockSize, n);
                        const size_t columnPanelToken =
                            columnBlock * blockCount + diagonalBlock;
                        const size_t targetToken =
                            rowBlock * blockCount + columnBlock;

                        if (rowBlock == columnBlock) {
#pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd, rowBegin, rowEnd,  \
                              columnBegin, columnEnd, rowPanelToken,         \
                              targetToken)                                  \
    shared(matrix, dependencyTokens, failureIndex)                           \
    depend(in : dependencyTokens[rowPanelToken])                             \
    depend(inout : dependencyTokens[targetToken])
                            updateTile(matrix, n, diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd,
                                       rowBegin, rowEnd, columnBegin,
                                       columnEnd, failureIndex);
                        } else {
#pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd, rowBegin, rowEnd,  \
                              columnBegin, columnEnd, rowPanelToken,         \
                              columnPanelToken, targetToken)                 \
    shared(matrix, dependencyTokens, failureIndex)                           \
    depend(in : dependencyTokens[rowPanelToken],                             \
           dependencyTokens[columnPanelToken])                              \
    depend(inout : dependencyTokens[targetToken])
                            updateTile(matrix, n, diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd,
                                       rowBegin, rowEnd, columnBegin,
                                       columnEnd, failureIndex);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    const size_t badDiagonal = failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (badDiagonal != n) {
        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element "
               "%zu\n",
               badDiagonal);
        return false;
    }

    // Zeroing is independent of the numerical factorization and scales well
    // enough to include all threads without adding synchronization to tasks.
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
    }

    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T.  Parallelizing by output row also first-touches A
    // across the participating NUMA nodes before the timed factorization.
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;

#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    // Reconstruct and compare in one pass.  L is triangular, so entries past
    // min(i,j) are known zeros and need not be read or multiplied.
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;

#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : maxError, relError)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double reconstructed = 0.0;
            const size_t last = std::min(i, j) + 1;

#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : reconstructed)
            for (size_t k = 0; k < last; ++k) {
                reconstructed += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }

            const size_t index = i * n + j;
            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - A_orig[index]);
            maxError = std::max(maxError, error);

            const double rel = error / (std::fabs(A_orig[index]) + 1e-10);
            relError = std::max(relError, rel);
        }
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {

+// A tile is large enough to amortize OpenMP task overhead while keeping the
+// three tiles used by an update in private cache on current multicore CPUs.
+constexpr size_t kBlockSize = 96;
+
+inline double dotProduct(const double* const left, const double* const right,
+                         const size_t count) {
+    size_t p = 0;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    // Four independent accumulators hide FMA latency.  Unaligned loads are
+    // intentional because tile rows need not begin on a vector boundary.
+    __m256d sum0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    for (; p + 16 <= count; p += 16) {
+        sum0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p), sum0);
+        sum1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 4),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 4), sum1);
+        sum2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 8),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 8), sum2);
+        sum3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 12),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 12), sum3);
+    }
 
+    const __m256d vectorSum =
+        _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_add_pd(sum0, sum1), _mm256_add_pd(sum2, sum3));
+    const __m128d halves =
+        _mm_add_pd(_mm256_castpd256_pd128(vectorSum),
+                   _mm256_extractf128_pd(vectorSum, 1));
+    sum = _mm_cvtsd_f64(_mm_hadd_pd(halves, halves));
+#endif
+
+    const size_t tailBegin = p;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+    for (size_t tail = tailBegin; tail < count; ++tail) {
+        sum += left[tail] * right[tail];
+    }
+    return sum;
+}
+
+// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
+// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
+void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
+                        const size_t end,
+                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+            size_t noFailure = n;
+            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
+                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        rowJ[j] = std::sqrt(value);
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];
+
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+            const double dot =
+                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
+// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
+void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                    const size_t rowEnd,
+                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          j - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}

+// A(rowBlock, columnBlock) -= L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) *

+// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.

+                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
+                const size_t columnEnd,

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

 #include <algorithm>

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
+
+// A tile is large enough to amortize OpenMP task overhead while keeping the
+// three tiles used by an update in private cache on current multicore CPUs.
+constexpr size_t kBlockSize = 96;
+
+inline double dotProduct(const double* const left, const double* const right,
+                         const size_t count) {
+    size_t p = 0;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    // Four independent accumulators hide FMA latency.  Unaligned loads are
+    // intentional because tile rows need not begin on a vector boundary.
+    __m256d sum0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    for (; p + 16 <= count; p += 16) {
+        sum0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p), sum0);
+        sum1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 4),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 4), sum1);
+        sum2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 8),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 8), sum2);
+        sum3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 12),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 12), sum3);
+    }
 
+    const __m256d vectorSum =
+        _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_add_pd(sum0, sum1), _mm256_add_pd(sum2, sum3));
+    const __m128d halves =
+        _mm_add_pd(_mm256_castpd256_pd128(vectorSum),
+                   _mm256_extractf128_pd(vectorSum, 1));
+    sum = _mm_cvtsd_f64(_mm_hadd_pd(halves, halves));
+#endif
+
+    const size_t tailBegin = p;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+    for (size_t tail = tailBegin; tail < count; ++tail) {
+        sum += left[tail] * right[tail];
+    }
+    return sum;
+}
+
+// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
+// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
+void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
+                        const size_t end,
+                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+            size_t noFailure = n;
+            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
+                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        rowJ[j] = std::sqrt(value);
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];
+
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+            const double dot =
+                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
+// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
+void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                    const size_t rowEnd,
+                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          j - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// A(rowBlock, columnBlock) -= L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) *
+// L(columnBlock, diagonalBlock)^T.  Only the lower half of diagonal tiles is
+// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.
+void updateTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
+                const size_t columnEnd,
+                const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(i + 1, columnEnd)
+                                               : columnEnd;
+        for (size_t j = columnBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          diagonalEnd - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] -= dot;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace

The tuned 96×96 tiles are the best balance on this host: they preserve enough task-level concurrency while raising the 2048, 64-thread result to about 115 GFLOP/s. Result hashes are identical between one and sixteen threads, confirming the dependency graph is deterministic; I’m doing the final sanitizer/clean-build checks now.

+// three tiles used by an update in private cache on current multicore CPUs.
+constexpr size_t kBlockSize = 96;
+
+inline double dotProduct(const double* const left, const double* const right,
+                         const size_t count) {
+    size_t p = 0;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    // Four independent accumulators hide FMA latency.  Unaligned loads are
+    // intentional because tile rows need not begin on a vector boundary.
+    __m256d sum0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    for (; p + 16 <= count; p += 16) {
+        sum0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p), sum0);
+        sum1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 4),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 4), sum1);
+        sum2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 8),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 8), sum2);
+        sum3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 12),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 12), sum3);
+    }
+
+    const __m256d vectorSum =
+        _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_add_pd(sum0, sum1), _mm256_add_pd(sum2, sum3));
+    const __m128d halves =
+        _mm_add_pd(_mm256_castpd256_pd128(vectorSum),
+                   _mm256_extractf128_pd(vectorSum, 1));
+    sum = _mm_cvtsd_f64(_mm_hadd_pd(halves, halves));
+#endif
+
+    const size_t tailBegin = p;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+    for (size_t tail = tailBegin; tail < count; ++tail) {
+        sum += left[tail] * right[tail];
+    }
+    return sum;
+}
+
+// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
+// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
+void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
+                        const size_t end,
+                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+            size_t noFailure = n;
+            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
+                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        rowJ[j] = std::sqrt(value);
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];
+
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+            const double dot =
+                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
+// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
+void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                    const size_t rowEnd,
+                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          j - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// A(rowBlock, columnBlock) -= L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) *
+// L(columnBlock, diagonalBlock)^T.  Only the lower half of diagonal tiles is
+// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.
+void updateTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
+                const size_t columnEnd,
+                const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(i + 1, columnEnd)
+                                               : columnEnd;
+        for (size_t j = columnBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          diagonalEnd - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] -= dot;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace

+// columns to execute concurrently while preserving update order for each tile.

+// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
+// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
+void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
+                        const size_t end,
+                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+            size_t noFailure = n;
+            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
+                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        rowJ[j] = std::sqrt(value);
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];
+
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+            const double dot =
+                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }

+// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
+// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
+void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                    const size_t rowEnd,
+                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          j - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}

+// L(columnBlock, diagonalBlock)^T.  Only the lower half of diagonal tiles is
+// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.
+void updateTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
+                const size_t columnEnd,
+                const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {

+        return;

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
 
+// A tile is large enough to amortize OpenMP task overhead while keeping the
+// three tiles used by an update in private cache on current multicore CPUs.
+constexpr size_t kBlockSize = 96;
+
+inline double dotProduct(const double* const left, const double* const right,
+                         const size_t count) {
+    size_t p = 0;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    // Four independent accumulators hide FMA latency.  Unaligned loads are
+    // intentional because tile rows need not begin on a vector boundary.
+    __m256d sum0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    for (; p + 16 <= count; p += 16) {
+        sum0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p), sum0);
+        sum1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 4),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 4), sum1);
+        sum2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 8),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 8), sum2);
+        sum3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 12),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 12), sum3);
+    }
+
+    const __m256d vectorSum =
+        _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_add_pd(sum0, sum1), _mm256_add_pd(sum2, sum3));
+    const __m128d halves =
+        _mm_add_pd(_mm256_castpd256_pd128(vectorSum),
+                   _mm256_extractf128_pd(vectorSum, 1));
+    sum = _mm_cvtsd_f64(_mm_hadd_pd(halves, halves));
+#endif
+
+    const size_t tailBegin = p;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+    for (size_t tail = tailBegin; tail < count; ++tail) {
+        sum += left[tail] * right[tail];
+    }
+    return sum;
+}
+
+// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
+// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
+void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
+                        const size_t end,
+                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+            size_t noFailure = n;
+            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
+                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        rowJ[j] = std::sqrt(value);
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];
+
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+            const double dot =
+                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
+// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
+void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                    const size_t rowEnd,
+                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          j - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// A(rowBlock, columnBlock) -= L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) *
+// L(columnBlock, diagonalBlock)^T.  Only the lower half of diagonal tiles is
+// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.
+void updateTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
+                const size_t columnEnd,
+                const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(i + 1, columnEnd)
+                                               : columnEnd;
+        for (size_t j = columnBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          diagonalEnd - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] -= dot;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace

+namespace {
 
+// A tile is large enough to amortize OpenMP task overhead while keeping the
+// three tiles used by an update in private cache on current multicore CPUs.
+constexpr size_t kBlockSize = 96;
+
+inline double dotProduct(const double* const left, const double* const right,
+                         const size_t count) {
+    size_t p = 0;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    // Four independent accumulators hide FMA latency.  Unaligned loads are
+    // intentional because tile rows need not begin on a vector boundary.
+    __m256d sum0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    for (; p + 16 <= count; p += 16) {
+        sum0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p), sum0);
+        sum1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 4),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 4), sum1);
+        sum2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 8),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 8), sum2);
+        sum3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 12),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 12), sum3);
+    }
+
+    const __m256d vectorSum =
+        _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_add_pd(sum0, sum1), _mm256_add_pd(sum2, sum3));
+    const __m128d halves =
+        _mm_add_pd(_mm256_castpd256_pd128(vectorSum),
+                   _mm256_extractf128_pd(vectorSum, 1));
+    sum = _mm_cvtsd_f64(_mm_hadd_pd(halves, halves));
+#endif
+
+    const size_t tailBegin = p;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+    for (size_t tail = tailBegin; tail < count; ++tail) {
+        sum += left[tail] * right[tail];
+    }
+    return sum;
+}
+
+// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
+// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
+void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
+                        const size_t end,
+                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+            size_t noFailure = n;
+            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
+                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        rowJ[j] = std::sqrt(value);
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];
+
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+            const double dot =
+                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
+// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
+void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                    const size_t rowEnd,
+                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          j - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// A(rowBlock, columnBlock) -= L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) *
+// L(columnBlock, diagonalBlock)^T.  Only the lower half of diagonal tiles is
+// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.
+void updateTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
+                const size_t columnEnd,
+                const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(i + 1, columnEnd)
+                                               : columnEnd;
+        for (size_t j = columnBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          diagonalEnd - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] -= dot;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace

+// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
+// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
+void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
+                        const size_t end,
+                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+            size_t noFailure = n;
+            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
+                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);

+

+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];

+            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+            const double dot =
+                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }

+// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
+// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
+void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                    const size_t rowEnd,
+                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          j - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
+        }

+// A(rowBlock, columnBlock) -= L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) *
+// L(columnBlock, diagonalBlock)^T.  Only the lower half of diagonal tiles is
+// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.
+void updateTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
+                const size_t columnEnd,
+                const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(i + 1, columnEnd)
+                                               : columnEnd;
+        for (size_t j = columnBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          diagonalEnd - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] -= dot;
+        }
+    }
+}
+

-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {
 
+// A tile is large enough to amortize OpenMP task overhead while keeping the
+// three tiles used by an update in private cache on current multicore CPUs.
+constexpr size_t kBlockSize = 96;
+
+inline double dotProduct(const double* const left, const double* const right,
+                         const size_t count) {
+    size_t p = 0;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    // Four independent accumulators hide FMA latency.  Unaligned loads are
+    // intentional because tile rows need not begin on a vector boundary.
+    __m256d sum0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    for (; p + 16 <= count; p += 16) {
+        sum0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p), sum0);
+        sum1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 4),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 4), sum1);
+        sum2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 8),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 8), sum2);
+        sum3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 12),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 12), sum3);
+    }
+
+    const __m256d vectorSum =
+        _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_add_pd(sum0, sum1), _mm256_add_pd(sum2, sum3));
+    const __m128d halves =
+        _mm_add_pd(_mm256_castpd256_pd128(vectorSum),
+                   _mm256_extractf128_pd(vectorSum, 1));
+    sum = _mm_cvtsd_f64(_mm_hadd_pd(halves, halves));
+#endif
+
+    const size_t tailBegin = p;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+    for (size_t tail = tailBegin; tail < count; ++tail) {
+        sum += left[tail] * right[tail];
+    }
+    return sum;
+}
+
+// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
+// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
+void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
+                        const size_t end,
+                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+            size_t noFailure = n;
+            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
+                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        rowJ[j] = std::sqrt(value);
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];
+
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+            const double dot =
+                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
+// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
+void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                    const size_t rowEnd,
+                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          j - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// A(rowBlock, columnBlock) -= L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) *
+// L(columnBlock, diagonalBlock)^T.  Only the lower half of diagonal tiles is
+// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.
+void updateTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
+                const size_t columnEnd,
+                const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(i + 1, columnEnd)
+                                               : columnEnd;
+        for (size_t j = columnBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          diagonalEnd - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] -= dot;
+        }
+    }
+}
+

    20	// A tile is large enough to amortize OpenMP task overhead while keeping the
    21	// three tiles used by an update in private cache on current multicore CPUs.
    22	constexpr size_t kBlockSize = 96;
    23	
    24	inline double dotProduct(const double* const left, const double* const right,
    25	                         const size_t count) {
    26	    size_t p = 0;
    27	    double sum = 0.0;
    28	
    29	#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
    30	    // Four independent accumulators hide FMA latency.  Unaligned loads are
    31	    // intentional because tile rows need not begin on a vector boundary.
    32	    __m256d sum0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    33	    __m256d sum1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    34	    __m256d sum2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    35	    __m256d sum3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
    36	    for (; p + 16 <= count; p += 16) {
    37	        sum0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p),
    38	                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p), sum0);
    39	        sum1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 4),
    40	                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 4), sum1);
    41	        sum2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 8),
    42	                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 8), sum2);
    43	        sum3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 12),
    44	                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 12), sum3);
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    const __m256d vectorSum =
    48	        _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_add_pd(sum0, sum1), _mm256_add_pd(sum2, sum3));
    49	    const __m128d halves =
    50	        _mm_add_pd(_mm256_castpd256_pd128(vectorSum),
    51	                   _mm256_extractf128_pd(vectorSum, 1));
    52	    sum = _mm_cvtsd_f64(_mm_hadd_pd(halves, halves));
    53	#endif
    54	
    55	    const size_t tailBegin = p;
    56	#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
    57	    for (size_t tail = tailBegin; tail < count; ++tail) {
    58	        sum += left[tail] * right[tail];
    59	    }
    60	    return sum;
    61	}
    62	
    63	// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
    64	// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
    65	void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
    66	                        const size_t end,
    67	                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
    68	    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
    69	        return;
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
    73	        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
    74	        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
    75	
    76	        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
    77	        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
    78	            size_t noFailure = n;
    79	            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
    80	                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    81	            return;
    82	        }
    83	
    84	        rowJ[j] = std::sqrt(value);
    85	        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];
    86	
    87	        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
    88	            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
    89	            const double dot =
    90	                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
    91	            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
    92	        }
    93	    }
    94	}
    95	
    96	// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
    97	// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
    98	void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
    99	                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
   100	                    const size_t rowEnd,
   101	                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
   102	    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
   103	        return;
   104	    }
   105	
   106	    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
   107	        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
   108	        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
   109	            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
   110	            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
   111	                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
   112	                                          j - diagonalBegin);
   113	            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
   114	        }
   115	    }
   116	}
   117	
   118	// A(rowBlock, columnBlock) -= L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) *
   119	// L(columnBlock, diagonalBlock)^T.  Only the lower half of diagonal tiles is
   120	// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.
   121	void updateTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
   122	                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
   123	                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
   124	                const size_t columnEnd,
   125	                const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
   126	    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
   127	        return;
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == columnBegin;
   131	    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
   132	        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
   133	        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(i + 1, columnEnd)
   134	                                               : columnEnd;
   135	        for (size_t j = columnBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
   136	            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
   137	            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
   138	                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
   139	                                          diagonalEnd - diagonalBegin);
   140	            rowI[j] -= dot;
   141	        }
   142	    }
   143	}
   144	
   145	}  // namespace
   146	
   147	// Blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  Explicit dependency tokens
   148	// describe the tile dataflow to OpenMP, allowing updates from different block
   149	// columns to execute concurrently while preserving update order for each tile.
   150	bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
   151	    if (n == 0) {
   152	        return true;
   153	    }
   154	
   155	    double* const matrix = A.data();
   156	    const size_t blockCount = (n + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize;
   157	    std::vector<unsigned char> tokenStorage(blockCount * blockCount);
   158	    [[maybe_unused]] unsigned char* const dependencyTokens =
   159	        tokenStorage.data();
   160	    std::atomic<size_t> failureIndex(n);
   161	
   162	#pragma omp parallel
   163	    {
   164	#pragma omp single
   165	        {
   166	            for (size_t diagonalBlock = 0; diagonalBlock < blockCount;
   167	                 ++diagonalBlock) {
   168	                const size_t diagonalBegin = diagonalBlock * kBlockSize;
   169	                const size_t diagonalEnd =
   170	                    std::min(diagonalBegin + kBlockSize, n);
   171	                const size_t diagonalToken =
   172	                    diagonalBlock * blockCount + diagonalBlock;
   173	
   174	#pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd, diagonalToken)      \
   175	    shared(matrix, dependencyTokens, failureIndex)                           \
   176	    depend(inout : dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) priority(2)
   177	                factorDiagonalTile(matrix, n, diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd,
   178	                                   failureIndex);
   179	
   180	                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
   181	                     rowBlock < blockCount; ++rowBlock) {
   182	                    const size_t rowBegin = rowBlock * kBlockSize;
   183	                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBegin + kBlockSize, n);
   184	                    const size_t panelToken =
   185	                        rowBlock * blockCount + diagonalBlock;
   186	
   187	#pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd, rowBegin, rowEnd,  \
   188	                              diagonalToken, panelToken)                     \
   189	    shared(matrix, dependencyTokens, failureIndex)                           \
   190	    depend(in : dependencyTokens[diagonalToken])                             \
   191	    depend(inout : dependencyTokens[panelToken]) priority(1)
   192	                    solvePanelTile(matrix, n, diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd,
   193	                                   rowBegin, rowEnd, failureIndex);
   194	                }
   195	
   196	                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
   197	                     rowBlock < blockCount; ++rowBlock) {
   198	                    const size_t rowBegin = rowBlock * kBlockSize;
   199	                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(rowBegin + kBlockSize, n);
   200	                    const size_t rowPanelToken =
   201	                        rowBlock * blockCount + diagonalBlock;
   202	
   203	                    for (size_t columnBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
   204	                         columnBlock <= rowBlock; ++columnBlock) {
   205	                        const size_t columnBegin = columnBlock * kBlockSize;
   206	                        const size_t columnEnd =
   207	                            std::min(columnBegin + kBlockSize, n);
   208	                        const size_t columnPanelToken =
   209	                            columnBlock * blockCount + diagonalBlock;
   210	                        const size_t targetToken =
   211	                            rowBlock * blockCount + columnBlock;
   212	
   213	                        if (rowBlock == columnBlock) {
   214	#pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd, rowBegin, rowEnd,  \
   215	                              columnBegin, columnEnd, rowPanelToken,         \
   216	                              targetToken)                                  \
   217	    shared(matrix, dependencyTokens, failureIndex)                           \
   218	    depend(in : dependencyTokens[rowPanelToken])                             \
   219	    depend(inout : dependencyTokens[targetToken])
   220	                            updateTile(matrix, n, diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd,
   221	                                       rowBegin, rowEnd, columnBegin,
   222	                                       columnEnd, failureIndex);
   223	                        } else {
   224	#pragma omp task firstprivate(diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd, rowBegin, rowEnd,  \
   225	                              columnBegin, columnEnd, rowPanelToken,         \
   226	                              columnPanelToken, targetToken)                 \
   227	    shared(matrix, dependencyTokens, failureIndex)                           \
   228	    depend(in : dependencyTokens[rowPanelToken],                             \
   229	           dependencyTokens[columnPanelToken])                              \
   230	    depend(inout : dependencyTokens[targetToken])
   231	                            updateTile(matrix, n, diagonalBegin, diagonalEnd,
   232	                                       rowBegin, rowEnd, columnBegin,
   233	                                       columnEnd, failureIndex);
   234	                        }
   235	                    }
   236	                }
   237	            }
   238	        }
   239	    }
   240	
   241	    const size_t badDiagonal = failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
   242	    if (badDiagonal != n) {
   243	        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element "
   244	               "%zu\n",
   245	               badDiagonal);
   246	        return false;
   247	    }
   248	
   249	    // Zeroing is independent of the numerical factorization and scales well
   250	    // enough to include all threads without adding synchronization to tasks.
   251	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   252	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   253	        std::fill(matrix + i * n + i + 1, matrix + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
   254	    }
   255	
   256	    return true;
   257	}
   258	
   259	// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
   260	void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
   261	    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
   262	    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
   263	    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
   264	    
   265	    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
   266	    unsigned int seed = 42;
   267	    
   268	    // Generate random matrix B
   269	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
   270	        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
   271	    }
   272	    
   273	    // Compute A = B * B^T.  Parallelizing by output row distributes the cubic
   274	    // setup work and the writes across the participating threads.
   275	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   276	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   277	        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
   278	            double sum = 0.0;
   279	
   280	#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
   281	            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
   282	                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
   283	            }
   284	            A[i * n + j] = sum;
   285	        }
   286	    }
   287	    
   288	    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
   289	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   290	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   291	        A[i * n + i] += n;
   292	    }
   293	}
   294	
   295	bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
   296	    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
   297	    
   298	    // Reconstruct and compare in one pass.  L is triangular, so entries past
   299	    // min(i,j) are known zeros and need not be read or multiplied.
   300	    double maxError = 0.0;
   301	    double relError = 0.0;
   302	
   303	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : maxError, relError)
   304	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   305	        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
   306	            double reconstructed = 0.0;
   307	            const size_t last = std::min(i, j) + 1;
   308	
   309	#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : reconstructed)
   310	            for (size_t k = 0; k < last; ++k) {
   311	                reconstructed += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
   312	            }
   313	
   314	            const size_t index = i * n + j;
   315	            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - A_orig[index]);
   316	            maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
   317	
   318	            const double rel = error / (std::fabs(A_orig[index]) + 1e-10);
   319	            relError = std::max(relError, rel);
   320	        }
   321	    }
   322	    
   323	    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
   324	    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
   325	    
   326	    // Check if error is within tolerance
   327	    if (relError > 1e-6) {
   328	        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
   329	        return false;
   330	    }
   331	    
   332	    return true;
   333	}

   338	    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
   339	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   340	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");

+constexpr size_t kBlockSize = 96;

+inline double dotProduct(const double* const left, const double* const right,
+                         const size_t count) {
+    size_t p = 0;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)
+    // Four independent accumulators hide FMA latency.  Unaligned loads are
+    // intentional because tile rows need not begin on a vector boundary.
+    __m256d sum0 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum1 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum2 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    __m256d sum3 = _mm256_setzero_pd();
+    for (; p + 16 <= count; p += 16) {
+        sum0 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p), sum0);
+        sum1 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 4),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 4), sum1);
+        sum2 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 8),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 8), sum2);
+        sum3 = _mm256_fmadd_pd(_mm256_loadu_pd(left + p + 12),
+                               _mm256_loadu_pd(right + p + 12), sum3);
+    }
+
+    const __m256d vectorSum =
+        _mm256_add_pd(_mm256_add_pd(sum0, sum1), _mm256_add_pd(sum2, sum3));
+    const __m128d halves =
+        _mm_add_pd(_mm256_castpd256_pd128(vectorSum),
+                   _mm256_extractf128_pd(vectorSum, 1));
+    sum = _mm_cvtsd_f64(_mm_hadd_pd(halves, halves));
+#endif
+
+    const size_t tailBegin = p;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+    for (size_t tail = tailBegin; tail < count; ++tail) {
+        sum += left[tail] * right[tail];
+    }
+    return sum;
+}
+
+// Factor the diagonal tile in place.  Earlier block products have already
+// been subtracted from it by updateTile().
+void factorDiagonalTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t begin,
+                        const size_t end,
+                        std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+        const double sum = dotProduct(rowJ + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (value <= 0.0 || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+            size_t noFailure = n;
+            failureIndex.compare_exchange_strong(
+                noFailure, j, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        rowJ[j] = std::sqrt(value);
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / rowJ[j];
+
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+            const double dot =
+                dotProduct(rowI + begin, rowJ + begin, j - begin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Solve L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) * L(diagonalBlock,
+// diagonalBlock)^T = A(rowBlock, diagonalBlock).
+void solvePanelTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                    const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                    const size_t rowEnd,
+                    const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = diagonalBegin; j < diagonalEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          j - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - dot) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// A(rowBlock, columnBlock) -= L(rowBlock, diagonalBlock) *
+// L(columnBlock, diagonalBlock)^T.  Only the lower half of diagonal tiles is
+// touched, so the upper triangle never participates in the factorization.
+void updateTile(double* const A, const size_t n, const size_t diagonalBegin,
+                const size_t diagonalEnd, const size_t rowBegin,
+                const size_t rowEnd, const size_t columnBegin,
+                const size_t columnEnd,
+                const std::atomic<size_t>& failureIndex) {
+    if (failureIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != n) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(i + 1, columnEnd)
+                                               : columnEnd;
+        for (size_t j = columnBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            const double dot = dotProduct(rowI + diagonalBegin,
+                                          rowJ + diagonalBegin,
+                                          diagonalEnd - diagonalBegin);
+            rowI[j] -= dot;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace
